

// Names this cartridge uses for its own address while a shared source uses it for another.
// A placement is a linker-script assignment and an assignment overrides an object, so
// leaving these alone relocated this cartridge's own references without failing.
// 33 respelt, derived from this cartridge's own disassembly.
eu_D_800C8610 = 0x800C8610; // absolute:True
eu_D_800C8660 = 0x800C8660; // absolute:True
eu_D_800C9118 = 0x800C9118; // absolute:True
eu_D_800CC930 = 0x800CC930; // absolute:True
eu_func_802BA4B0 = 0x802BA4B0; //

// ---- shared-spelling disambiguations; maintained by hand, no command writes this file ----
// Nothing in the toolkit writes this file: every reference to [split].symbols is a read. The
// line here used to say this block was "rewritten on every run", which described a migration
// command that does not exist -- the rewriting has been done each time from scratch scripts a
// lane wrote for itself and then threw away. A sentence asserting behaviour nothing implements
// is the same defect as an address no other table can contradict, so it is corrected rather
// than left standing. Edits below are ordinary hand edits and are reviewed as such.
// Names this cartridge does not derive from its own addresses.
//
// One authored tree serves all five cartridges, and a name in it is derived from the reference
// cartridge's address. The same code is almost never at the same place, so the shared spelling is
// given this cartridge's address in the split below, and this cartridge's own interval at the
// literal address the spelling reads as takes the disambiguated spelling here -- the C can no
// longer be rewritten, so the inversion is forced. An interval whose derived name is the stem of a
// shared source it is not paired with is disambiguated for the same reason: left alone, that source
// would displace code it is not.

// 2698 shared name(s) at this cartridge's own address. Stated here because the
// splitter takes a function's name from this table and not from the subsegment's
// file name: renaming the subsegment alone left the interval's assembly defining
// the derived spelling while the shared C defined the shared one, so every caller
// in this cartridge's assembly reached the link undefined.
